                        <script>
                            (function () {
                                var root = document.getElementById("gpch-1-tabs");
                                if (!root) { return; }
                                var tabs   = root.querySelectorAll('.gp-tab[data-tab]');
                                var panels = root.querySelectorAll('.gp-tablewrap[data-panel]');
                                tabs.forEach(function (tab) {
                                    tab.addEventListener('click', function () {
                                        var active = tab.dataset.tab;
                                        panels.forEach(function (p) {
                                            p.style.display = p.dataset.panel === active ? '' : 'none';
                                        });
                                        tabs.forEach(function (t) { t.classList.toggle('is-active', t === tab); });
                                    });
                                });
                            }());
                        </script>

                        <script>
                            (function () {
                                var root = document.getElementById("gpch-2-tabs");
                                if (!root) { return; }
                                var tabs   = root.querySelectorAll('.gp-tab[data-tab]');
                                var panels = root.querySelectorAll('.gp-tablewrap[data-panel]');
                                tabs.forEach(function (tab) {
                                    tab.addEventListener('click', function () {
                                        var active = tab.dataset.tab;
                                        panels.forEach(function (p) {
                                            p.style.display = p.dataset.panel === active ? '' : 'none';
                                        });
                                        tabs.forEach(function (t) { t.classList.toggle('is-active', t === tab); });
                                    });
                                });
                            }());
                        </script>
                    </div>

                        <script>
                            (function () {
                                var root = document.getElementById("gpch-3-tabs");
                                if (!root) { return; }
                                var tabs   = root.querySelectorAll('.gp-tab[data-tab]');
                                var panels = root.querySelectorAll('.gp-tablewrap[data-panel]');
                                tabs.forEach(function (tab) {
                                    tab.addEventListener('click', function () {
                                        var active = tab.dataset.tab;
                                        panels.forEach(function (p) {
                                            p.style.display = p.dataset.panel === active ? '' : 'none';
                                        });
                                        tabs.forEach(function (t) { t.classList.toggle('is-active', t === tab); });
                                    });
                                });
                            }());
                        </script>
                    </div>

                        <script>
                            (function () {
                                var root = document.getElementById("gpch-4-tabs");
                                if (!root) { return; }
                                var tabs   = root.querySelectorAll('.gp-tab[data-tab]');
                                var panels = root.querySelectorAll('.gp-tablewrap[data-panel]');
                                tabs.forEach(function (tab) {
                                    tab.addEventListener('click', function () {
                                        var active = tab.dataset.tab;
                                        panels.forEach(function (p) {
                                            p.style.display = p.dataset.panel === active ? '' : 'none';
                                        });
                                        tabs.forEach(function (t) { t.classList.toggle('is-active', t === tab); });
                                    });
                                });
                            }());
                        </script>
